Null values\n// are ignored. The first type in the order empty, integer, number, date,\n// boolean, text that the non-null values all satisfy is returned. Classification\n// never uses float parsing and never rewrites a value.\nfunc InferType(values []string) string {\n\tvar present []string\n\tfor _, value := range values {\n\t\tif !csvdata.IsNull(value) {\n\t\t\tpresent = append(present, value)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tswitch {\n\tcase len(present) == 0:\n\t\treturn TypeEmpty\n\tcase allMatch(present, integerPattern.MatchString):\n\t\treturn TypeInteger\n\tcase allMatch(present, numberPattern.MatchString):\n\t\treturn TypeNumber\n\tcase allMatch(present, isDate):\n\t\treturn TypeDate\n\tcase allMatch(present, isBoolean):\n\t\treturn TypeBoolean\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn TypeText\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc allMatch(values []string, match func(string) bool) bool {\n\tfor _, value := range values {\n\t\tif !match(value) {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn true\n}\n\n// isDate requires the YYYY-MM-DD shape and a real calendar date.\nfunc isDate(value string) bool {\n\tif !datePattern.MatchString(value) {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\t_, err := time.Parse(\"2006-01-02\", value)\n\treturn err == nil\n}\n\nfunc isBoolean(value string) bool {\n\treturn strings.EqualFold(value, \"true\") || strings.EqualFold(value, \"false\")\n}\n----VALIDATE\npackage csvdata\n\nimport (\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n)\n\n// ErrRejected matches every input rejection with errors.Is.\nvar ErrRejected = errors.New(\"input rejected\")\n\n// RejectedError describes why an input was rejected. Line is the 1-based source\n// line of the offending record, or 0 when the rejection concerns the header or\n// the whole input.\ntype RejectedError struct {\n\tLine   int\n\tReason string\n}\n\nfunc (e *RejectedError) Error() string {\n\tif e.Line == 0 {\n\t\treturn e.Reason\n\t}\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"line %d: %s\", e.Line, e.Reason)\n}\n\n// Unwrap makes errors.Is(err, ErrRejected) true for every RejectedError.\nfunc (e *RejectedError) Unwrap() error {\n\treturn ErrRejected\n}\n\n// Validate rejects input that must produce no output: no header record (empty\n// or blank-only input), an empty or repeated header name, and any record whose\n// field count differs from the header. Call it after Trim, so names compare as\n// trimmed. A header-only table is valid.\nfunc Validate(t *Table) error {\n\tif t.Header == nil {\n\t\treturn &RejectedError{Reason: \"no header record: input is empty or blank\"}\n\t}\n\n\tseen := make(map[string]bool, len(t.Header))\n\tfor i, name := range t.Header {\n\t\tif name == \"\" {\n\t\t\treturn &RejectedError{Reason: fmt.Sprintf(\"header column %d is empty\", i+1)}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif seen[name] {\n\t\t\treturn &RejectedError{Reason: fmt.Sprintf(\"header name %q is repeated\", name)}\n\t\t}\n\t\tseen[name] = true\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, rec := range t.Records {\n\t\tif len(rec.Fields) != len(t.Header) {\n\t\t\treturn &RejectedError{\n\t\t\t\tLine:   rec.Line,\n\t\t\t\tReason: fmt.Sprintf(\"record has %d fields, header has %d\", len(rec.Fields), len(t.Header)),\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n----READ\npackage csvdata\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/csv\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n)\n\n// Record is one data record and the 1-based source line on which it starts.\ntype Record struct {\n\tLine   int\n\tFields []string\n}\n\n// Table is a parsed CSV input: the header record and the data records after it.\ntype Table struct {\n\tHeader  []string\n\tRecords []Record\n}\n\n// Read parses r as RFC 4180 CSV.
